{"parentUuid":"1f8dd6fb-f964-4cb0-a39f-813e0d8b1999","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d56cacaa-c9dc-4e85-ae04-66fc75b89de3","agentId":"abb5bc5cd2dfafb95","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WX1NPmGDkDMsGEXJgkDEPC","type":"tool_result","content":"total 8\ndrwxr-xr-x@ 4 quochoangdev  staff  128 Sep 26 08:41 .\ndrwxr-xr-x@ 7 quochoangdev  staff  224 Sep 27 15:33 ..\ndrwxr-xr-x@ 5 quochoangdev  staff  160 Sep 24 12:11 environment\n-rw-r--r--@ 1 quochoangdev  staff  915 Sep 27 14:55 instruction.md\nDispatch runs the bakery's wholesale deliveries from plans made by `/app/planner/plan_morning.py`, and those plans cost far too much; on two of the next four mornings they do not even reach every customer. Replace them. For each morning in `/app/orders/` (`harbour-lanes`, `old-town`, `riverside` and `north-ring`), write a plan to `/app/plans/<morning>.json` that obeys the dispatch rules in `/app/docs/dispatch-rules.md` and costs no more than that morning's target in `/app/orders/targets.json`. Each target is the most the dispatch office will pay for that morning.\n\n`/app/tools/check_plan.py` applies the rules to a plan and prints its cost, and the rules document gives the plan format. {"parentUuid":"8650a1fa-2efa-44f6-b741-fdbb032ee3db","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d56cacaa-c9dc-4e85-ae04-66fc75b89de3","agentId":"abb5bc5cd2dfafb95","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","content":"Exit code 1\n# Morning dispatch rules\n\nThese rules decide whether a delivery plan can be run and what it costs. They\nare the rules `tools/check_plan.py` applies.\n\n## Orders\n\nEach file in `orders/` is one morning. It lists the bakery (`depot`), the\ncustomers to deliver to, and the vehicles available (`fleet`). Positions are\ninteger grid coordinates `x`, `y` in units of 100 m; times are seconds after\nmidnight. The bakery opens at `open` and closes at `close`.\n\nA customer has an integer `id`, a position, a `demand` in crates, a delivery\nwindow `ready`..`due`, and a `service` time in seconds for unloading. Its\n`kind` (cafe, grocer, hotel, market) is for information only and changes no\nrule.\n\nA fleet entry is one kind of vehicle, named by its `type`: `count` vehicles of it are available,\neach carrying up to `capacity` crates. It travels at `pace` seconds per unit of\ndistance, costs `fixed` for being used at all and `per_unit` for every unit of\ndistance it drives, may be out for at most `shift` seconds from leaving the\nbakery to getting back, and, where `range` is not null, may drive at most\n`range` units of distance in the morning. Costs are in cents.\n\n## Distance and time\n\nThe distance between two points is the Euclidean distance between them,\nrounded up to a whole unit. A vehicle takes `distance * pace` seconds to drive\nit.\n\n## A route\n\nA route is one vehicle's morning: it leaves the bakery at its departure time,\nvisits its customers in order, and comes back. Every route visits at least one\ncustomer, and each vehicle runs at most one route.\n\n- The departure is a whole number of seconds, not before the bakery opens.\n- At each customer the vehicle unloads from the later of its arrival and the\n  customer's `ready` time, and that must not be after `due`. Unloading takes the\n  customer's `service` time; the vehicle leaves as soon as it is done.\n- The vehicle must be back at the bakery no later than it closes, and no more\n  than `shift` seconds after its departure.\n- The crates on the route (the sum of its customers' demands) must fit the\n  vehicle's `capacity`, and the distance driven must be within its `range`\n  where it has one. The distance driven is the sum of the rounded legs, from\n  the bakery through each customer in turn and back to the bakery.\n\n## A plan\n\nA plan delivers to every customer of the morning exactly once, uses no more\nvehicles of a kind than the fleet has, and costs the sum over its routes of the\nvehicle's `fixed` cost plus `per_unit` times the route's distance.\n\nIt is written as JSON:\n\n    {\"routes\": [{\"type\": \"van\", \"depart\": 18000, \"stops\": [12, 4, 31]}, ...]}\n\nwhere `type` names a fleet entry, `depart` is the departure time as a JSON\ninteger (`18000`, not `18000.0`) and `stops` lists customer ids, as the\nintegers in the orders file, in visiting order. The plan object has no key\nbesides `routes`, a route has no keys besides these three, and no object\nrepeats a key.\n(eval):1: ==== not found","is_error":true,"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YPr1QcmbaK6Zu4o8m6Laae"}]},"uuid":"6ed1b4ce-d40a-4530-91ce-24886c5607d0","timestamp":"2026-09-27T08:33:37.166Z","toolUseResult":"Error: Exit code 1\n# Morning dispatch rules\n\nThese rules decide whether a delivery plan can be run and what it costs. Unloading takes the\n  customer's `service` time; the vehicle leaves as soon as it is done.\n- The vehicle must be back at the bakery no later than it closes, and no more\n  than `shift` seconds after its departure.\n- The crates on the route (the sum of its customers' demands) must fit the\n  vehicle's `capacity`, and the distance driven must be within its `range`\n  where it has one. The distance driven is the sum of the rounded legs, from\n  the bakery through each customer in turn and back to the bakery.\n\n## A plan\n\nA plan delivers to every customer of the morning exactly once, uses no more\nvehicles of a kind than the fleet has, and costs the sum over its routes of the\nvehicle's `fixed` cost plus `per_unit` times the route's distance.\n\nIt is written as JSON:\n\n    {\"routes\": [{\"type\": \"van\", \"depart\": 18000, \"stops\": [12, 4, 31]}, ...]}\n\nwhere `type` names a fleet entry, `depart` is the departure time as a JSON\ninteger (`18000`, not `18000.0`) and `stops` lists customer ids, as the\nintegers in the orders file, in visiting order. The plan object has no key\nbesides `routes`, a route has no keys besides these three, and no object\nrepeats a key.\n(eval):1: ==== not found","serverClassifierContext":{"request":"b9c3e7a4-4077-4564-b5e1-75463cf5debd","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/Users/quochoangdev/HDTechLS/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3","root":"/Users/quochoangdev/HDTechLS/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":2,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":0},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"HDTech-Labs/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3","visibility":"private"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"personal","host":"github.com","remote":"quochoangidx/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3-cloud","visibility":"unknown"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"HDTech-Labs/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3","visibility":"private"}]}},"live_cwd":"/Users/quochoangdev/HDTechLS/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3","platform":"macos"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8650a1fa-2efa-44f6-b741-fdbb032ee3db","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/quochoangdev/HDTechLS/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3","sessionId":"2a178ccc-047d-4550-99cf-18e2cae87650","version":"2.1.281","gitBranch":"main"}

sys\n\n\nclass PlanError(ValueError):\n    pass\n\n\nROUTE_KEYS = {\"type\", \"depart\", \"stops\"}\n\n\ndef _no_repeats(pairs):\n    keys = [k for k, _ in pairs]\n    if len(set(keys)) != len(keys):\n        raise PlanError(f\"a key appears twice in one object: {sorted(k for k in set(keys) if keys.count(k) > 1)}\")\n    return dict(pairs)\n\n\ndef _not_json(name):\n    raise PlanError(f\"{name} is not a JSON value\")\n\n\ndef load_plan(path):\n    \"\"\"Read a plan file as strict JSON: no repeated keys, no NaN or Infinity.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        with open(path, encoding=\"utf-8\") as fh:\n            return json.load(fh, object_pairs_hook=_no_repeats, parse_constant=_not_json)\n    except PlanError:\n        raise\n    except (ValueError, RecursionError) as exc:\n        raise PlanError(f\"not a readable JSON plan: {exc}\") from None\n\n\ndef distance(a, b):\n    sq = (a[\"x\"] - b[\"x\"]) ** 2 + (a[\"y\"] - b[\"y\"]) ** 2\n    d = math.isqrt(sq)\n    return d if d * d == sq else d + 1\n\n\ndef evaluate(orders, plan):\n    depot = orders[\"depot\"]\n    customers = {c[\"id\"]: c for c in orders[\"customers\"]}\n    fleet = {f[\"type\"]: f for f in orders[\"fleet\"]}\n    used = {t: 0 for t in fleet}\n    served = set()\n    total = 0\n    if not isinstance(plan, dict) or not isinstance(plan.get(\"routes\"), list):\n        raise PlanError(\"a plan is a JSON object with a routes list\")\n    if set(plan) != {\"routes\"}:\n        raise PlanError(f\"a plan has only a routes key, not {sorted(set(plan) - {'routes'})}\")\n    for number, route in enumerate(plan[\"routes\"], start=1):\n        where = f\"route {number}\"\n        if not isinstance(route, dict):\n            raise PlanError(f\"{where}: not a JSON object\")\n        if set(route) != ROUTE_KEYS:\n            raise PlanError(f\"{where}: a route has exactly the keys type, depart and stops\")\n        kind = fleet.get(route.get(\"type\")) if isinstance(route.get(\"type\"), str) else None\n        if kind is None:\n            raise PlanError(f\"{where}: unknown vehicle type {route.get('type')!r}\")\n        stops = route.get(\"stops\")\n        if not isinstance(stops, list) or not stops:\n            raise PlanError(f\"{where}: no stops\")\n        depart = route.get(\"depart\")\n        if not isinstance(depart, int) or isinstance(depart, bool) or depart < depot[\"open\"]:\n            raise PlanError(f\"{where}: departure must be a whole second, not before opening\")\n        used[route[\"type\"]] += 1\n        clock, here, crates, driven = depart, depot, 0, 0\n        for cid in stops:\n            if not isinstance(cid, int) or isinstance(cid, bool) or cid not in customers:\n                raise PlanError(f\"{where}: unknown customer {cid!r}\")\n            if cid in served:\n                raise PlanError(f\"{where}: customer {cid} delivered twice\")\n            served.add(cid)\n            customer = customers[cid]\n            leg = distance(here, customer)\n            driven += leg\n            start = max(clock + leg * kind[\"pace\"], customer[\"ready\"])\n            if start > customer[\"due\"]:\n                raise PlanError(f\"{where}: customer {cid} reached at {start}, after its window closes at {customer['due']}\")\n            clock = start + customer[\"service\"]\n            crates += customer[\"demand\"]\n            here = customer\n        leg = distance(here, depot)\n        driven += leg\n        back = clock + leg * kind[\"pace\"]\n        if back > depot[\"close\"]:\n            raise PlanError(f\"{where}: back at {back}, after the bakery closes\")\n        if back - depart > kind[\"shift\"]:\n            raise PlanError(f\"{where}: out for {back - depart} s, longer than the {kind['shift']} s shift\")\n        if crates > kind[\"capacity\"]:\n            raise PlanError(f\"{where}: {crates} crates on a vehicle that carries {kind['capacity']}\")\n        if kind[\"range\"] is not None and driven > kind[\"range\"]:\n            raise PlanError(f\"{where}: drives {driven} units, beyond its range of {kind['range']}\")\n        total += kind[\"fixed\"] + kind[\"per_unit\"] * driven\n    for name, count in used.items():\n        if count > fleet[name][\"count\"]:\n            raise PlanError(f\"{count} routes use a {name}; only {fleet[name]['count']} are available\")\n    missing = sorted(set(customers) - served)\n    if missing:\n        raise PlanError(f\"{len(missing)} customers get no delivery, first {missing[:5]}\")\n    return total\n\n\ndef main(argv):\n    if len(argv) != 3:\n        sys.exit(__doc__)\n    with open(argv[1]) as fh:\n        orders = json.load(fh)\n    try:\n        cost = evaluate(orders, load_plan(argv[2]))\n    except PlanError as exc:\n        print(f\"INFEASIBLE: {exc}\")\n        return 1\n    print(f\"feasible, cost {cost}\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n {"parentUuid":"e10fcc38-bfe7-40ff-94e7-fd8bbb2c38ed","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"abb5bc5cd2dfafb95","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CfTdq6AZVQGLdrCsqQg9M","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01LAtZRaq7hwC2pp9mUcpaAh","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/quochoangdev/HDTechLS/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3/workspace/local-solve-probes/tbrain-bakery-fleet-dispatch/run_1/solve/environment/app/planner/solve_lns.py","content":"\"\"\"Large-neighbourhood-search planner for the morning dispatch problem.\n\nUsage: python3 planner/solve_lns.py orders/<m>.json plans/<m>.json <seconds> [seed]\n\nOnly the Python 3 standard library is used.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport math\nimport random\nimport sys\nimport time\n\n\ndef dist(a, b):\n    sq = (a[0] - b[0]) ** 2 + (a[1] - b[1]) ** 2\n    d = math.isqrt(sq)\n    return d if d * d == sq else d + 1\n\n\nclass Problem:\n    def __init__(self, orders):\n        depot = orders[\"depot\"]\n        self.open = depot[\"open\"]\n        self.close = depot[\"close\"]\n        cs = orders[\"customers\"]\n        self.n = len(cs)\n        self.ids = [0] + [c[\"id\"] for c in cs]\n        pts = [(depot[\"x\"], depot[\"y\"])] + [(c[\"x\"], c[\"y\"]) for c in cs]\n        self.pts = pts\n        m = len(pts)\n        self.D = [[0] * m for _ in range(m)]\n        for i in range(m):\n            Di = self.D[i]\n            pi = pts[i]\n            for j in range(m):\n                Di[j] = dist(pi, pts[j])\n        self.dem = [0] + [c[\"demand\"] for c in cs]\n        self.ready = [self.open] + [c[\"ready\"] for c in cs]\n        self.due = [self.close] + [c[\"due\"] for c in cs]\n        self.srv = [0] + [c[\"service\"] for c in cs]\n        self.fleet = orders[\"fleet\"]\n        self.types = [f[\"type\"] for f in self.fleet]\n        self.F = {f[\"type\"]: f for f in self.fleet}\n        # neighbour lists (by index 1..n)\n        K = min(30, self.n - 1)\n        self.nbr = [[] for _ in range(m)]\n        for i in range(1, m):\n            order = sorted(range(1, m), key=lambda j: self.D[i][j])\n            self.nbr[i] = [j for j in order if j != i][:K]\n\n\nclass Route:\n    __slots__ = (\"vt\", \"stops\", \"load\", \"dist\", \"e\", \"l\", \"cost\", \"depart\", \"ok\")\n\n    def __init__(self, vt, stops):\n        self.vt = vt\n        self.stops = stops\n\n\nclass Solver:\n    def __init__(self, prob, seed=0):\n        self.p = prob\n        self.rng = random.Random(seed)\n\n    # ---------- route evaluation ----------\n    def full_eval(self, vt, stops):\n        \"\"\"Return (dist, depart, cost) or None when infeasible.\"\"\"\n        p = self.p\n        f = p.F[vt]\n        pace = f[\"pace\"]\n        load = 0\n        for s in stops:\n            load += p.dem[s]\n        if load > f[\"capacity\"]:\n            return None\n        D = p.D\n        d = D[0][stops[0]] + D[stops[-1]][0]\n        for a, b in zip(stops, stops[1:]):\n            d += D[a][b]\n        rng = f[\"range\"]\n        if rng is not None and d > rng:\n            return None\n        latest = p.close\n        nxt = 0\n        for c in reversed(stops):\n            latest = min(p.due[c], latest - p.srv[c] - D[c][nxt] * pace)\n            if latest < p.ready[c]:\n                return None\n            nxt = c\n        depart = latest - D[0][stops[0]] * pace\n        if depart < p.open:\n            return None\n        clock = depart\n        here = 0\n        for c in stops:\n            st = clock + D[here][c] * pace\n            if st < p.ready[c]:\n                st = p.ready[c]\n            if st > p.due[c]:\n                return None\n            clock = st + p.srv[c]\n            here = c\n        back = clock + D[here][0] * pace\n        if back > p.close or back - depart > f[\"shift\"]:\n            return None\n        return d, depart, f[\"fixed\"] + f[\"per_unit\"] * d\n\n    def refresh(self, r):\n        \"\"\"Recompute cached arrays for a feasible route.\"\"\"\n        p = self.p\n        f = p.F[r.vt]\n        pace = f[\"pace\"]\n        D = p.D\n        stops = r.stops\n        res = self.full_eval(r.vt, stops)\n        if res is None:\n            r.ok = False\n            return False\n        r.dist, r.depart, r.cost = res\n        r.load = sum(p.dem[s] for s in stops)\n        # earliest starts with depart = open\n        e = []\n        clock = p.open\n        here = 0\n        for c in stops:\n            st = clock + D[here][c] * pace\n            if st < p.ready[c]:\n                st = p.ready[c]\n            e.append(st)\n            clock = st + p.srv[c]\n            here = c\n        # latest starts\n        l = [0] * len(stops)\n        latest = p.close\n        nxt = 0\n        for i in range(len(stops) - 1, -1, -1):\n            c = stops[i]\n            latest = min(p.due[c], latest - p.srv[c] - D[c][nxt] * pace)\n            l[i] = latest\n            nxt = c\n        r.e = e\n        r.l = l\n        r.ok = True\n        return True\n\n    # ---------- solution bookkeeping ----------\n    def sol_cost(self, routes):\n        return sum(r.cost for r in routes)\n\n    def used_counts(self, routes):\n        u = {t: 0 for t in self.p.types}\n        for r in routes:\n            u[r.vt] += 1\n        return u\n\n    # ---------- insertion ----------\n    def insertion_candidates(self, u, routes, where):\n        \"\"\"Yield (delta_cost, route_index, position) for feasible-looking spots.\"\"\"\n        p = self.p\n        D = p.D\n        du = p.due[u]\n        ru = p.ready[u]\n        su = p.srv[u]\n        demu = p.dem[u]\n        out = []\n        seen = set()\n        for r_i, pos_list in where.items():\n            r = routes[r_i]\n            f = p.F[r.vt]\n            if r.load + demu > f[\"capacity\"]:\n                continue\n            pace = f[\"pace\"]\n            pu = f[\"per_unit\"]\n            rng = f[\"range\"]\n            stops = r.stops\n            e = r.e\n            l = r.l\n            L = len(stops)\n            for pos in pos_list:\n                key = (r_i, pos)\n                if key in seen:\n                    continue\n                seen.add(key)\n                prev = stops[pos - 1] if pos > 0 else 0\n                nxt = stops[pos] if pos < L else 0\n                delta_d = D[prev][u] + D[u][nxt] - D[prev][nxt]\n                if rng is not None and r.dist + delta_d > rng:\n                    continue\n                fin_prev = p.open if pos == 0 else e[pos - 1] + p.srv[prev]\n                st = fin_prev + D[prev][u] * pace\n                if st < ru:\n                    st = ru\n                if st > du:\n                    continue\n                lat_next = l[pos] if pos < L else p.close\n                if st + su + D[u][nxt] * pace > lat_next:\n                    continue\n                out.append((pu * delta_d, r_i, pos))\n        return out\n\n    def new_route_options(self, u, used):\n        p = self.p\n        best = []\n        for t in p.types:\n            if used[t] >= p.F[t][\"count\"]:\n                continue\n            res = self.full_eval(t, [u])\n            if res is None:\n                continue\n            best.append((res[2], t))\n        best.sort()\n        return best\n\n    def where_map(self, u, routes, pos_of):\n        \"\"\"Candidate positions near u: around its nearest assigned neighbours.\"\"\"\n        where = {}\n        for v in self.p.nbr[u]:\n            loc = pos_of.get(v)\n            if loc is None:\n                continue\n            r_i, idx = loc\n            where.setdefault(r_i, set()).update((idx, idx + 1))\n        return where\n\n    def repair(self, routes, pool, used, noise=0.0):\n        \"\"\"Regret-2 insertion of customers in pool. Returns False on failure.\"\"\"\n        p = self.p\n        pos_of = {}\n        for r_i, r in enumerate(routes):\n            for i, c in enumerate(r.stops):\n                pos_of[c] = (r_i, i)\n        pool = list(pool)\n        rng = self.rng\n        while pool:\n            best_u = None\n            best_score = None\n            best_move = None\n            for u in pool:\n                where = self.where_map(u, routes, pos_of)\n                cands = self.insertion_candidates(u, routes, where)\n                nr = self.new_route_options(u, used)\n                opts = []\n                for c, r_i, pos in cands:\n                    if noise:\n                        c += rng.uniform(0, noise)\n                    opts.append((c, (\"ins\", r_i, pos)))\n                if nr:\n                    c = nr[0][0]\n                    if noise:\n                        c += rng.uniform(0, noise)\n                    opts.append((c, (\"new\", nr[0][1], 0)))\n                    if len(nr) > 1:\n                        opts.append((nr[1][0], (\"new\", nr[1][1], 0)))\n                if not opts:\n                    return False\n                opts.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])\n                first = opts[0]\n                second = opts[1][0] if len(opts) > 1 else first[0] + 100000\n                regret = second - first[0]\n                score = (regret, -first[0])\n                if best_score is None or score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_u = u\n                    best_move = first[1]\n            u = best_u\n            kind, a, b = best_move\n            placed = False\n            if kind == \"ins\":\n                r = routes[a]\n                old = r.stops\n                r.stops = old[:b] + [u] + old[b:]\n                if self.refresh(r):\n                    placed = True\n                else:\n                    r.stops = old\n                    self.refresh(r)\n            if not placed:\n                if kind == \"ins\":\n                    # try new route instead\n                    nr = self.new_route_options(u, used)\n                    if not nr:\n                        return False\n                    a = nr[0][1]\n                r = Route(a, [u])\n                if not self.refresh(r):\n                    return False\n                routes.append(r)\n                used[a] += 1\n            pool.remove(u)\n            pos_of = {}\n            for r_i, rr in enumerate(routes):\n                for i, c in enumerate(rr.stops):\n                    pos_of[c] = (r_i, i)\n        return True\n\n    # ---------- improvement ----------\n    def retype(self, routes, used):\n        \"\"\"Give each route the cheapest feasible vehicle type.\"\"\"\n        p = self.p\n        changed = False\n        for r in routes:\n            best = (r.cost, r.vt)\n            for t in p.types:\n                if t == r.vt:\n                    continue\n                if used[t] >= p.F[t][\"count\"]:\n                    continue\n                res = self.full_eval(t, r.stops)\n                if res is None:\n                    continue\n                if res[2] < best[0]:\n                    best = (res[2], t)\n            if best[1] != r.vt:\n                used[r.vt] -= 1\n                used[best[1]] += 1\n                r.vt = best[1]\n                self.refresh(r)\n                changed = True\n        return changed\n\n    def intra_opt(self, r):\n        \"\"\"Or-opt and 2-opt inside one route.\"\"\"\n        improved = True\n        any_change = False\n        while improved:\n            improved = False\n            L = len(r.stops)\n            if L < 3:\n                break\n            base = r.cost\n            for i in range(L - 1):\n                for j in range(i + 2, L + 1):\n                    new = r.stops[:i] + r.stops[i:j][::-1] + r.stops[j:]\n                    res = self.full_eval(r.vt, new)\n                    if res is not None and res[2] < base - 1e-9:\n                        r.stops = new\n                        self.refresh(r)\n                        base = r.cost\n                        improved = True\n                        any_change = True\n                        break\n                if improved:\n                    break\n            if improved:\n                continue\n            for seg in (1, 2, 3):\n                for i in range(L - seg + 1):\n                    piece = r.stops[i:i + seg]\n                    rest = r.stops[:i] + r.stops[i + seg:]\n                    for k in range(len(rest) + 1):\n                        if k == i:\n                            continue\n                        new = rest[:k] + piece + rest[k:]\n                        res = self.full_eval(r.vt, new)\n                        if res is not None and res[2] < base - 1e-9:\n                            r.stops = new\n                            self.refresh(r)\n                            base = r.cost\n                            improved = True\n                            any_change = True\n                            break\n                    if improved:\n                        break\n                if improved:\n                    break\n        return any_change\n\n    # ---------- removal ----------\n    def remove(self, routes, q):\n        p = self.p\n        rng = self.rng\n        assigned = [(c, r_i) for r_i, r in enumerate(routes) for c in r.stops]\n        if not assigned:\n            return []\n        op = rng.random()\n        pool = set()\n        if op < 0.30:\n            for c, _ in rng.sample(assigned, min(q, len(assigned))):\n                pool.add(c)\n        elif op < 0.75:\n            seed = rng.choice(assigned)[0]\n            order = [seed] + [v for v in p.nbr[seed]]\n            in_sol = {c for c, _ in assigned}\n            for v in order:\n                if len(pool) >= q:\n                    break\n                if v in in_sol:\n                    pool.add(v)\n            while len(pool) < q and len(pool) < len(assigned):\n                pool.add(rng.choice(assigned)[0])\n        else:\n            idxs = list(range(len(routes)))\n            rng.shuffle(idxs)\n            for r_i in idxs:\n                if len(pool) >= q:\n                    break\n                pool.update(routes[r_i].stops)\n        for r in routes:\n            r.stops = [c for c in r.stops if c not in pool]\n        return list(pool)\n\n    def clean(self, routes, used):\n        keep = []\n        for r in routes:\n            if r.stops:\n                keep.append(r)\n            else:\n                used[r.vt] -= 1\n        return keep\n\n    def copy_routes(self, routes):\n        out = []\n        for r in routes:\n            nr = Route(r.vt, list(r.stops))\n            nr.load = r.load\n            nr.dist = r.dist\n            nr.e = r.e\n            nr.l = r.l\n            nr.cost = r.cost\n            nr.depart = r.depart\n            nr.ok = r.ok\n            out.append(nr)\n        return out\n\n    # ---------- main ----------\n    def solve(self, tlimit):\n        p = self.p\n        deadline = time.time() + tlimit\n        routes = []\n        used = {t: 0 for t in p.types}\n        pool = list(range(1, p.n + 1))\n        # seed order: tight windows first\n        pool.sort(key=lambda c: (p.due[c], -p.dem[c]))\n        if not self.repair(routes, pool, used):\n            raise SystemExit(\"construction failed\")\n        routes = self.clean(routes, used)\n        self.retype(routes, used)\n        for r in routes:\n            self.intra_opt(r)\n        cur = routes\n        cur_cost = self.sol_cost(cur)\n        best = self.copy_routes(cur)\n        best_cost = cur_cost\n        best_used = dict(used)\n        cur_used = dict(used)\n        T0 = max(1.0, 0.012 * cur_cost / max(1, p.n))\n        it = 0\n        last_report = time.time()\n        while time.time() < deadline:\n            it += 1\n            frac = 1.0 - (deadline - time.time()) / tlimit\n            T = T0 * (0.02 ** frac)\n            cand = self.copy_routes(cur)\n            cand_used = dict(cur_used)\n            q = self.rng.randint(max(2, p.n // 50), max(4, p.n // 6))\n            pool = self.remove(cand, q)\n            cand = self.clean(cand, cand_used)\n            for r in cand:\n                if not self.refresh(r):\n                    break\n            noise = self.rng.choice([0.0, 0.0, 30.0, 120.0])\n            ok = self.repair(cand, pool, cand_used, noise)\n            if not ok:\n                continue\n            cand = self.clean(cand, cand_used)\n            if self.rng.random() < 0.25:\n                self.retype(cand, cand_used)\n            if self.rng.random() < 0.35:\n                for r in cand:\n                    if len(r.stops) > 2:\n                        self.intra_opt(r)\n            c = self.sol_cost(cand)\n            if c < cur_cost or self.rng.random() < math.exp(-(c - cur_cost) / max(T, 1e-9)):\n                cur = cand\n                cur_cost = c\n                cur_used = cand_used\n                if c < best_cost:\n                    best_cost = c\n                    best = self.copy_routes(cand)\n                    best_used = dict(cand_used)\n            if time.time() - last_report > 20:\n                last_report = time.time()\n                print(f\"  it={it} best={best_cost} cur={cur_cost}\", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n        # final polish\n        for r in best:\n            self.intra_opt(r)\n        self.retype(best, best_used)\n        for r in best:\n            self.intra_opt(r)\n        best_cost = self.sol_cost(best)\n        return best, best_cost, it\n\n\ndef to_plan(prob, routes):\n    out = []\n    for r in routes:\n        out.append({\n            \"type\": r.vt,\n            \"depart\": int(r.depart),\n            \"stops\": [int(prob.ids[s]) for s in r.stops],\n        })\n    return {\"routes\": out}\n\n\ndef main(argv):\n    orders_path, plan_path, tl = argv[1], argv[2], float(argv[3])\n    seed = int(argv[4]) if len(argv) > 4 else 12345\n    with open(orders_path) as fh:\n        orders = json.load(fh)\n    prob = Problem(orders)\n    best = None\n    best_cost = None\n    # multi-start within the budget\n    starts = 3 if prob.n <= 70 else 2\n    slice_t = tl / starts\n    for k in range(starts):\n        s = Solver(prob, seed + 977 * k)\n        routes, cost, it = s.solve(slice_t)\n        print(f\"start {k}: cost {cost} after {it} iterations\", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n        if best_cost is None or cost < best_cost:\n            best, best_cost = routes, cost\n    plan = to_plan(prob, best)\n    with open(plan_path, \"w\") as fh:\n        json.dump(plan, fh)\n    print(f\"{orders_path}: cost {best_cost}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main(sys.argv)\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1876,"cache_read_input_tokens":10773,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1876,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01LAtZRaq7hwC2pp9mUcpaAh":{"file_path":"/Users/quochoangdev/HDTechLS/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3/workspace/local-solve-probes/tbrain-bakery-fleet-dispatch/run_1/solve/environment/app/planner/solve_lns.py","content":"\"\"\"Large-neighbourhood-search json\nimport math\nimport random\nimport sys\nimport time\n\n\ndef dist(a, b):\n    sq = (a[0] - b[0]) ** 2 + (a[1] - b[1]) ** 2\n    d = math.isqrt(sq)\n    return d if d * d == sq else d + 1\n\n\nclass Problem:\n    def __init__(self, orders):\n        depot = orders[\"depot\"]\n        self.open = depot[\"open\"]\n        self.close = depot[\"close\"]\n        cs = orders[\"customers\"]\n        self.n = len(cs)\n        self.ids = [0] + [c[\"id\"] for c in cs]\n        pts = [(depot[\"x\"], depot[\"y\"])] + [(c[\"x\"], c[\"y\"]) for c in cs]\n        self.pts = pts\n        m = len(pts)\n        self.D = [[0] * m for _ in range(m)]\n        for i in range(m):\n            Di = self.D[i]\n            pi = pts[i]\n            for j in range(m):\n                Di[j] = dist(pi, pts[j])\n        self.dem = [0] + [c[\"demand\"] for c in cs]\n        self.ready = [self.open] + [c[\"ready\"] for c in cs]\n        self.due = [self.close] + [c[\"due\"] for c in self.fleet}\n        # neighbour lists (by index 1..n)\n        K = min(30, self.n - 1)\n        self.nbr = [[] for _ in range(m)]\n        for i in range(1, m):\n            order = sorted(range(1, m), key=lambda j: self.D[i][j])\n            self.nbr[i] = [j for j in order if j != i][:K]\n\n\nclass Route:\n    __slots__ = (\"vt\", \"stops\", \"load\", \"dist\", \"e\", \"l\", \"cost\", \"depart\", \"ok\")\n\n    def __init__(self, vt, stops):\n        self.vt = vt\n        self.stops = stops\n\n\nclass Solver:\n    def __init__(self, prob, seed=0):\n        self.p = prob\n        self.rng = random.Random(seed)\n\n    # ---------- route evaluation ----------\n    def full_eval(self, vt, stops):\n        \"\"\"Return (dist, depart, cost) or None when infeasible.\"\"\"\n        p = self.p\n        f = p.F[vt]\n        pace = f[\"pace\"]\n        load = 0\n        for s in stops:\n            load += p.dem[s]\n        if load > f[\"capacity\"]:\n            return None\n        D = p.D\n        d = D[0][stops[0]] + D[stops[-1]][0]\n        for a, b in zip(stops, stops[1:]):\n            d += D[a][b]\n        rng = f[\"range\"]\n        if rng is not None and d > rng:\n            return None\n        latest = p.close\n        nxt = 0\n        for c in reversed(stops):\n            latest = min(p.due[c], latest - p.srv[c] - D[c][nxt] * pace)\n            if latest < p.ready[c]:\n                return None\n            nxt = c\n        depart = latest - D[0][stops[0]] * pace\n        if depart < p.open:\n            return None\n        clock = depart\n        here = 0\n        for c in stops:\n            st = clock + D[here][c] * pace\n            if st < p.ready[c]:\n                st = p.ready[c]\n            if st > p.due[c]:\n                return None\n            clock = st + p.srv[c]\n            here = c\n        back = clock + D[here][0] * pace\n        if back > p.close or back - depart > f[\"shift\"]:\n            return None\n        return d, depart, f[\"fixed\"] + f[\"per_unit\"] * d\n\n    def refresh(self, r):\n        \"\"\"Recompute cached arrays for a feasible route.\"\"\"\n        p = self.p\n        f = p.F[r.vt]\n        pace = f[\"pace\"]\n        D = p.D\n        stops = r.stops\n        res = self.full_eval(r.vt, stops)\n        if res is None:\n            r.ok = False\n            return False\n        r.dist,            st = clock + D[here][c] * pace\n            if st < p.ready[c]:\n                st = p.ready[c]\n            e.append(st)\n            clock = st + p.srv[c]\n            here = c\n        # latest starts\n        l = [0] * len(stops)\n        latest = p.close\n        nxt = 0\n        for i in range(len(stops) - 1, -1, -1):\n            c = stops[i]\n            latest = min(p.due[c], bookkeeping ----------\n    def sol_cost(self, routes):\n        return sum(r.cost for r in routes)\n\n    def used_counts(self, routes):\n        u = {t: 0 for t in self.p.types}\n        for r in routes:\n            u[r.vt] += 1\n        return u\n\n    # ---------- insertion ----------\n    def insertion_candidates(self, u, routes, where):\n        \"\"\"Yield (delta_cost, route_index, position) for feasible-looking spots.\"\"\"\n        p = self.p\n        D = p.D\n        du = p.due[u]\n        ru = p.ready[u]\n        su = p.srv[u]\n        demu = p.dem[u]\n        out = []\n        seen = set()\n        for r_i, pos_list in where.items():\n            r = routes[r_i]\n            f = p.F[r.vt]\n            if r.load + demu > f[\"capacity\"]:\n                continue\n            pace = f[\"pace\"]\n            pu = f[\"per_unit\"]\n            rng = f[\"range\"]\n            stops = r.stops\n            e = r.e\n            l = r.l\n            L = len(stops)\n            for pos in pos_list:\n                key = (r_i, pos)\n                if key in seen:\n                    continue\n                seen.add(key)\n                prev = stops[pos - 1] if pos > 0 else 0\n                nxt = stops[pos] if pos < L else 0\n                delta_d = D[prev][u] + D[u][nxt] - D[prev][nxt]\n                if rng is not None and r.dist + delta_d > rng:\n                    continue\n                fin_prev = p.open if pos == 0 else e[pos - 1] + p.srv[prev]\n                st = fin_prev + D[prev][u] * pace\n                if st < ru:\n                    st = ru\n                if st > du:\n                    continue\n                lat_next = l[pos] if pos < L else p.close\n                if st + su + D[u][nxt] * pace > lat_next:\n                    continue\n                out.append((pu * delta_d, r_i, pos))\n        return out\n\n    def new_route_options(self, u, used):\n        p = self.p\n        best = []\n        for t in p.types:\n t))\n        best.sort()\n        return best\n\n    def where_map(self, u, routes, pos_of):\n        \"\"\"Candidate positions near u: around its nearest assigned neighbours.\"\"\"\n        where = {}\n        for v in self.p.nbr[u]:\n            loc = pos_of.get(v)\n            if loc is None:\n                continue\n            r_i, idx = loc\n            where.setdefault(r_i, set()).update((idx, idx + 1))\n        return where\n\n    def repair(self, routes, pool, used, noise=0.0):\n        \"\"\"Regret-2 insertion of customers in pool. Returns False on failure.\"\"\"\n        p = self.p\n        pos_of = {}\n        for r_i, r in enumerate(routes):\n            for i, c in enumerate(r.stops):\n                pos_of[c] = (r_i, i)\n        pool = list(pool)\n        rng = self.rng\n        while pool:\n            best_u = None\n            best_score = None\n            best_move = None\n            for u in pool:\n                where = self.where_map(u, routes, pos_of)\n                cands = self.insertion_candidates(u, routes, where)\n                nr = self.new_route_options(u, used)\n                opts = []\n                for c, r_i, pos in cands:\n                    if noise:\n                        c += rng.uniform(0, noise)\n                    opts.append((c, (\"ins\", r_i, pos)))\n                if nr:\n                    c = nr[0][0]\n                    if noise:\n                        c += rng.uniform(0, noise)\n                    opts.append((c, (\"new\", nr[0][1], 0)))\n                    if len(nr) > 1:\n                        opts.append((nr[1][0], (\"new\", nr[1][1], 0)))\n                if not opts:\n                    return False\n                opts.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])\n                first = opts[0]\n                second = opts[1][0] if len(opts) > 1 else first[0] + 100000\n                regret = second - first[0]\n                score = (regret, -first[0])\n                if best_score is None or score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_u = u\n                    best_move = first[1]\n            u = best_u\n            kind, a, b = best_move\n            placed = False\n            if kind == \"ins\":\n                r = routes[a]\n                old = r.stops\n                r.stops =    if not placed:\n                if kind == \"ins\":\n                    # try new route instead\n                    nr = self.new_route_options(u, used)\n                    if not nr:\n                        return False\n                    a = nr[0][1]\n                r = Route(a, [u])\n                if not self.refresh(r):\n                    return False\n                routes.append(r)\n                used[a] += 1\n            pool.remove(u)\n            pos_of = {}\n            for r_i, rr in enumerate(routes):\n                for i, c in enumerate(rr.stops):\n                    pos_of[c] = (r_i, i)\n        return True\n\n    # ---------- improvement ----------\n    def retype(self, routes, used):\n        \"\"\"Give each route the cheapest feasible vehicle type.\"\"\"\n        p = self.p\n        changed = False\n        for r in routes:\n            best = (r.cost, r.vt)\n            for t in p.types:\n                if t == r.vt:\n                    continue\n                if used[t] >= p.F[t][\"count\"]:\n                    continue\n                res = self.full_eval(t, r.stops)\n                if res is None:\n                    continue\n                if res[2] < best[0]:\n                    best = (res[2], t)\n            if best[1] != r.vt:\n                used[r.vt] -= 1\n                used[best[1]] += 1\n     improved = True\n        any_change = False\n        while improved:\n            improved = False\n            L = len(r.stops)\n            if L < 3:\n                break\n            base = r.cost\n            for i in range(L - 1):\n                for j in range(i + 2, L + 1):\n                    new = r.stops[:i] + r.stops[i:j][::-1] + r.stops[j:]\n                    res = self.full_eval(r.vt, new)\n                    if res is not None and res[2] < base - 1e-9:\n                        r.stops = new\n                        self.refresh(r)\n                        base = r.cost\n                        improved = True\n                        any_change = True\n                        break\n                if improved:\n                    break\n            if improved:\n                continue\n            for seg in (1, 2, 3):\n                for i in range(L - seg + 1):\n                    piece = r.stops[i:i + seg]\n                    rest = r.stops[:i] + r.stops[i + seg:]\n                    for k in range(len(rest) + 1):\n                        if k == i:\n                            continue\n                        new = rest[:k] + piece + rest[k:]\n                        res = self.full_eval(r.vt, new)\n                        if res is not None and res[2] < base - 1e-9:\n                            r.stops = new\n break\n                    if improved:\n                        break\n                if improved:\n                    break\n        return any_change\n\n    # ---------- removal ----------\n    def remove(self, routes, q):\n        p = self.p\n        rng = self.rng\n        assigned = [(c, r_i) for r_i, r in enumerate(routes) for c in r.stops]\n        if not assigned:\n            return []\n        op = rng.random()\n        pool = set()\n        if op < 0.30:\n            for c, _ in rng.sample(assigned, min(q, len(assigned))):\n                pool.add(c)\n        elif op < 0.75:\n            seed = rng.choice(assigned)[0]\n            order = [seed] + [v for v in p.nbr[seed]]\n            in_sol = {c for c, _ in assigned}\n            for v in order:\n                if len(pool) >= q:\n                    break\n                if v in in_sol:\n                    pool.add(v)\n            while len(pool) < q and len(pool) < len(assigned):\n                pool.add(rng.choice(assigned)[0])\n        else:\n            idxs = list(range(len(routes)))\n            rng.shuffle(idxs)\n            for r_i in idxs:\n                if len(pool) >= q:\n                    break\n                pool.update(routes[r_i].stops)\n        for r in routes:\n            r.stops = [c for c in r.stops if c not in pool]\n        return list(pool)\n\n    def keep\n\n    def copy_routes(self, routes):\n        out = []\n        for r in routes:\n            nr = Route(r.vt, list(r.stops))\n            nr.load = r.load\n            nr.dist = r.dist\n            nr.e = r.e\n            nr.l = r.l\n            nr.cost = r.cost\n            nr.depart = r.depart\n            nr.ok = r.ok\n            out.append(nr)\n        return out\n\n    # ---------- main ----------\n    def solve(self, tlimit):\n        p = self.p\n        deadline = time.time() + tlimit\n        routes = []\n        used = {t: 0 for t in p.types}\n        pool = list(range(1, p.n + 1))\n        # seed order: tight windows first\n        pool.sort(key=lambda c: (p.due[c], -p.dem[c]))\n        if not self.repair(routes, pool, used):\n            raise  cur_cost = self.sol_cost(cur)\n        best = self.copy_routes(cur)\n        best_cost = cur_cost\n        best_used = dict(used)\n        cur_used = dict(used)\n        T0 = max(1.0, 0.012 * cur_cost / max(1, p.n))\n        it = 0\n        last_report = time.time()\n        while time.time() < deadline:\n            it += 1\n            frac = 1.0 - (deadline - time.time()) / tlimit\n            T = T0 * (0.02 ** frac)\n            cand = self.copy_routes(cur)\n            cand_used = dict(cur_used)\n            q = self.rng.randint(max(2, p.n // 50), max(4, p.n // 6))\n            pool = self.remove(cand, q)\n            cand = self.clean(cand, cand_used)\n            for r in cand:\n                if not self.refresh(r):\n                    break\n            noise = self.rng.choice([0.0, 0.0, 30.0, 120.0])\n            ok = self.repair(cand, pool, cand_used, noise)\n            if not ok:\n                continue\n            cand = self.clean(cand, cand_used)\n            if self.rng.random() < 0.25:\n                self.retype(cand, cand_used)\n            if self.rng.random() < 0.35:\n                for r in cand:\n                    if len(r.stops) > 2:\n                        self.intra_opt(r)\n            c = self.sol_cost(cand)\n            if c < cur_cost or self.rng.random() < math.exp(-(c - cur_cost) / max(T, 1e-9)):\n                cur = cand\n                cur_cost = c\n                cur_used = cand_used\n                if c < best_cost:\n                    best_cost = c\n                    best = self.copy_routes(cand)\n {"parentUuid":"f390d7ac-d749-48a9-b5f4-25f11657eb02","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"abb5bc5cd2dfafb95","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CfTeABQrR9HnrBVcrg9Mj","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01HWvtTn4ZMaFoeTkd4KcM6W","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"/Users/quochoangdev/HDTechLS/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3/workspace/local-solve-probes/tbrain-bakery-fleet-dispatch/run_1/solve/environment/app/planner/solve_lns.py","old_string":"    def where_map(self, u, routes, pos_of):","new_string":"    def upgrade_options(self, u, routes, used):\n        \"\"\"Fallback: re-type a route to a bigger free vehicle so u fits in it.\"\"\"\n        p = self.p\n        out = []\n        for r_i, r in enumerate(routes):\n            for t in p.types:\n                if t == r.vt or used[t] >= p.F[t][\"count\"]:\n                    continue\n                if p.F[t][\"capacity\"] < r.load + p.dem[u]:\n                    continue\n                best = None\n                for pos in range(len(r.stops) + 1):\n                    new = r.stops[:pos] + [u] + r.stops[pos:]\n                    res = self.full_eval(t, new)\n                    if res is not None and (best is None or res[2] < best[0]):\n                        best = (res[2], pos)\n                if best is not None:\n                    out.append((best[0] - <<'PYEOF'\np='planner/solve_lns.py'\ns=open(p).read()\nstart = s.index('    # ---------- main ----------')\nend = s.index('def to_plan(')\nnew = '''    # ---------- main ----------\n    PEN = 200000\n\n    def solve(self, tlimit):\n        p = self.p\n        deadline = time.time() + tlimit\n        routes = []\n        used = {t: 0 for t in p.types}\n        pool = list(range(1, p.n + 1))\n        # seed order: tight windows first\n        pool.sort(key=lambda c: (p.due[c], -p.dem[c]))\n        un = self.repair(routes, pool, used)\n        routes = self.clean(routes, used)\n        self.retype(routes, used)\n        for r in routes:\n            self.intra_opt(r)\n        cur = routes\n        cur_un = un\n        cur_cost = self.sol_cost(cur) + self.PEN * len(cur_un)\n        best = self.copy_routes(cur)\n        best_un = list(cur_un)\n        best_cost = cur_cost\n        cur_used = dict(used)\n        best_used = dict(used)\n        T0 = max(1.0, 0.015 * self.sol_cost(cur) / max(1, p.n))\n        it = 0\n        last_report = time.time()\n        while time.time() < deadline:\n            it += 1\n            frac = 1.0 - (deadline - time.time()) / tlimit\n            T = T0 * (0.02 ** frac)\n            cand = self.copy_routes(cur)\n            cand_used = dict(cur_used)\n            q = self.rng.randint(max(2, p.n // 50), max(4, p.n // 6))\n            pool = self.remove(cand, q) + list(cur_un)\n            cand = self.clean(cand, cand_used)\n            noise = self.rng.choice([0.0, 0.0, 30.0, 120.0])\n            cand_un = self.repair(cand, pool, cand_used, noise)\n            cand = self.clean(cand, cand_used)\n            if self.rng.random() < 0.25:\n                self.retype(cand, cand_used)\n            if self.rng.random() < 0.35:\n                for r in cand:\n                    if len(r.stops) > 2:\n                        self.intra_opt(r)\n            c = self.sol_cost(cand) + self.PEN * len(cand_un)\n            if c < cur_cost or self.rng.random() < math.exp(-min(50.0, (c - cur_cost) / max(T, 1e-9))):\n                cur = cand\n                cur_cost = c\n                cur_un = cand_un\n                cur_used = cand_used\n                if c < best_cost:\n                    best_cost = c\n                    best = self.copy_routes(cand)\n                    best_un = list(cand_un)\n                    best_used = dict(cand_used)\n            if time.time() - last_report > 30:\n                last_report = time.time()\n                print(f\"  it={it} best={best_cost} un={len(best_un)}\", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n        # final polish\n        for r in best:\n            self.intra_opt(r)\n        self.retype(best, best_used)\n        for r in best:\n            self.intra_opt(r)\n        best_cost = self.sol_cost(best) + self.PEN * len(best_un)\n        return best, best_cost, it, len(best_un)\n\n\n'''\ns = s[:start] + new + s[end:]\ns = s.replace('''        routes, cost, it = s.solve(slice_t)\n        print(f\"start {k}: cost {cost} after {it} iterations\", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n        if best_cost is None or cost < best_cost:''','''        routes, cost, it, nun = s.solve(slice_t)\n        print(f\"start {k}: cost {cost} un={nun} after {it} iterations\", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n        if nun:\n            continue\n        if best_cost is None or cost < best_cost:''')\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nPYEOF\npython3 -c \"import ast;ast.parse(open('/Users/quochoangdev/HDTechLS/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3/workspace/local-solve-probes/tbrain-bakery-fleet-dispatch/run_1/solve/environment/app/planner/solve_lns.py').read())\" && echo OK"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1247,"cache_read_input_tokens":36225,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1247,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01EWBirQ7ENbyWEz133QVYEQ":{"command":"cd /Users/quochoangdev/HDTechLS/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3/workspace/local-solve-probes/tbrain-bakery-fleet-dispatch/run_1/solve/environment/app && python3 - {"parentUuid":"445756ed-dd3a-44d4-8fe0-1971521855db","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"abb5bc5cd2dfafb95","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CfTeeEXedanFxtQUP8954","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011hczb4J7i2nLfjFkjnqiqZ","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /Users/quochoangdev/HDTechLS/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3/workspace/local-solve-probes/tbrain-bakery-fleet-dispatch/run_1/solve/environment/app && python3 - <<'PYEOF'\np='planner/solve_lns.py'\ns=open(p).read()\nanchor='    # ---------- removal ----------'\nnew = '''    def inter_opt(self, routes, used, passes=2):\n        \"\"\"Relocate single customers between routes while cost falls.\"\"\"\n        p = self.p\n        D = p.D\n        for _ in range(passes):\n            changed = False\n            pos_of = {}\n            for r_i, r in enumerate(routes):\n                for i, c in enumerate(r.stops):\n                    pos_of[c] = (r_i, i)\n            order = list(pos_of)\n            self.rng.shuffle(order)\n            for u in order:\n                loc = pos_of.get(u)\n                if loc is None:\n                    continue\n                src_i, idx = loc\n                src = routes[src_i]\n                if src.stops[idx] != u:\n                    continue\n                rest = src.stops[:idx] + src.stops[idx + 1:]\n                if rest:\n                    res = self.full_eval(src.vt, rest)\n                    if res is None:\n                        continue\n                    gain = src.cost - {"parentUuid":"be317880-4c7f-4467-8329-cd294c52b39a","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"abb5bc5cd2dfafb95","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CfTghauZqMdQDW81dHrnz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013sGpjTEFKXhqbP1xJjtZpz","name":"SubagentHandback","input":{"message":"Done, with a partial result: all four plans are feasible, but only one is under its target.\n\n## Final checker output (`python3 tools/check_plan.py orders/<m>.json plans/<m>.json`)\n\n| morning | cost | target | status |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| harbour-lanes | 80012 | 80734 | under target |\n| old-town | 150490 | 148979 | 1.0% over |\n| riverside | 215072 | 210436 | 2.2% over |\n| north-ring | 303488 | 302006 | 0.5% over |\n\nAll four report `feasible` (every customer served once, windows, shift, capacity, range and fleet counts all respected).\n\n## Files written (absolute paths)\n- `/Users/quochoangdev/HDTechLS/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3/workspace/local-solve-probes/tbrain-bakery-fleet-dispatch/run_1/solve/environment/app/plans/harbour-lanes.json`\n- `.../environment/app/plans/old-town.json`\n- `.../environment/app/plans/riverside.json`\n- `.../environment/app/plans/north-ring.json`\n- `.../environment/app/planner/solve_lns.py` (new; the solver I wrote, stdlib only). No existing file was modified.\n\n## Approach\n`solve_lns.py` is an adaptive LNS for this heterogeneous-fleet VRPTW:\n- Exact route evaluator: backward pass gives the latest feasible departure (duration is non-increasing in departure time, so the latest feasible departure minimises the out-time and is the only departure worth testing against `shift`), then a forward pass verifies windows, close and shift.\n- Regret-2 insertion with O(1) window feasibility from cached earliest/latest-start arrays, candidate positions restricted to the 30 nearest neighbours, plus a \"re-type the route to a larger free vehicle\" fallback (needed: pure greedy construction runs out of vehicles on old-town, where fleet capacity 1656 vs demand 1282 is tight).\n- Unserved customers are carried with a 200000-cent penalty so construction never hard-fails.\n- Ruin operators: random, related (Shaw), whole-route. Repair with noise. Simulated-annealing acceptance.\n- Improvement: intra-route 2-opt/Or-opt, inter-route relocate, and cheapest-feasible-type reassignment.\n\n## Commands run\nTimed runs per morning (two processes in parallel, ~5–8 min each), e.g.\n`TFAC=0.00009 STARTS=2 python3 planner/solve_lns.py orders/riverside.json plans/riverside.json 300 31`\nthen `tools/check_plan.py` on every morning.\n\n## Uncertainty / what I would do next\n- The gap is search time, not structure: old-town went 156755 -> 153259 -> 150490 as run length grew, riverside 217248 -> 215072 just by lowering the SA temperature (`TFAC` env var; low, near-greedy temperatures clearly beat higher ones here). Longer runs, or several seeds per morning kept in parallel, should close the remaining 0.5–2.2%.\n- The main structural lever I did not implement is inter-route swap / 2-opt* and an explicit route-merge (e.g. folding van routes into a truck). Cost is fixed-cost dominated (old-town: 89400 fixed vs 67355 distance), so operators that eliminate a whole vehicle are worth more than distance polish.\n- I had a one-shot 60-minute budget and spent the last ~25 minutes on final solver runs, so there was no time left to re-run old-town or north-ring after the temperature finding."},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":494,"cache_read_input_tokens":52775,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":494,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_013sGpjTEFKXhqbP1xJjtZpz":{"message":"Done, with a partial result: all four plans are feasible, but only one is under its target.\n\n## Final checker output (`python3 tools/check_plan.py orders/<m>.json plans/<m>.json`)\n\n| morning | cost | target | status |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| harbour-lanes | 80012 | 80734 | under target |\n| old-town | 150490 | 148979 | 1.0% over |\n| riverside | 215072 | 210436 | 2.2% over |\n| north-ring | 303488 | 302006 | 0.5% over |\n\nAll four report `feasible` (every customer served once, windows, shift, capacity, range and fleet counts all respected).\n\n## Files written (absolute paths)\n- `/Users/quochoangdev/HDTechLS/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3/workspace/local-solve-probes/tbrain-bakery-fleet-dispatch/run_1/solve/environment/app/plans/harbour-lanes.json`\n- `.../environment/app/plans/old-town.json`\n- `.../environment/app/plans/riverside.json`\n- `.../environment/app/plans/north-ring.json`\n- `.../environment/app/planner/solve_lns.py` (new; the solver I wrote, stdlib only). No existing file was modified.\n\n## Approach\n`solve_lns.py` is an adaptive LNS for this heterogeneous-fleet VRPTW:\n- Exact route evaluator: backward pass gives the latest feasible departure (duration is non-increasing in departure time, so the latest feasible departure minimises the out-time and is the only departure worth testing against `shift`), then a forward pass verifies windows, close and shift.\n- Regret-2 insertion with O(1) window feasibility from cached earliest/latest-start arrays, candidate positions restricted to the 30 nearest neighbours, plus a \"re-type the route to a larger free vehicle\" fallback (needed: pure greedy construction runs out of vehicles on old-town, where fleet capacity 1656 vs demand 1282 is tight).\n- Unserved customers are carried with a 200000-cent penalty so construction never hard-fails.\n- Ruin operators: random, related (Shaw), whole-route. Repair with noise. Simulated-annealing acceptance.\n- Improvement: intra-route 2-opt/Or-opt, inter-route relocate, and cheapest-feasible-type reassignment.\n\n## Commands run\nTimed runs per morning (two processes in parallel, ~5–8 min each), e.g.\n`TFAC=0.00009 STARTS=2 python3 planner/solve_lns.py orders/riverside.json plans/riverside.json 300 31`\nthen `tools/check_plan.py` on every morning.\n\n## Uncertainty / what I would do next\n- The gap is search time, not structure: old-town went 156755 -> 153259 -> 150490 as run length grew, riverside 217248 -> 215072 just by lowering the SA temperature (`TFAC` env var; low, near-greedy temperatures clearly beat higher ones here). Longer runs, or several seeds per morning kept in parallel, should close the remaining 0.5–2.2%.\n- The main structural lever I did not implement is inter-route swap / 2-opt* and an explicit route-merge (e.g. folding van routes into a truck). Cost is fixed-cost dominated (old-town: 89400 fixed vs 67355 distance), so operators that eliminate a whole vehicle are worth more than distance polish.\n- I had a one-shot 60-minute budget and spent the last ~25 minutes on final solver runs, so there was no time left to re-run old-town or north-ring after the temperature finding."}},"serverClassifierRequest":"bf89a738-bded-4a29-a48e-0b2c31e1542f","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfTghaRoSQEmGyJYEvhKT","attributionAgent":"terminus-probe","attributionSkill":"task-local-solve-probe","type":"assistant","uuid":"ca23f0ec-0af3-4ad6-b099-2395c2d4d23c","timestamp":"2026-09-27T09:11:48.535Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":null,"userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/quochoangdev/HDTechLS/hd-labs-terminus-skills-v3","sessionId":"2a178ccc-047d-4550-99cf-18e2cae87650","version":"2.1.281","gitBranch":"main"}
